We present JamSet and JamBench, the first project-level game code framework dataset and benchmark built on a professional game engine. Our key insight is that Game Jam competitions, community events where developers build complete games under tight time constraints, yield thousands of open-source projects suitable for this purpose. Building on the Godot engine's text-based format and headless execution mode, we design a deterministic verification pipeline from file integrity to runtime behavior collection, distilling 8,133 verified projects from over 240,000 repositories. Of these, 300 manually verified projects form JamBench; the rest constitute JamSet. JamBench defines theme-driven generation and code completion tasks, evaluated through a pipeline combining compilation pass rates, Structural Completeness Score (SCS), and Behavioral Alignment Score (BAS). Evaluation of 9 frontier models reveals a capability cliff as project scale increases, with runtime pass rates dropping from 80.4% on small projects to 5.7% on large ones (Task2a). Code Agents improve compilation rates yet yield no gains in runtime behavioral quality, indicating that the bottleneck lies in architectural design rather than syntactic correctness. Experiments validate JamSet as effective training data. All data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [183] [Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation with Large Language Models: From Prompting to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19988)
*Shi Chen,Rongcun Wang,Yuan Tian,Xiaoyuan Xie,Wei Song,Rubing Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SolidityBench（5,470 个仓库级 Solidity 智能合约基准）和 SolidityScore（面向 Solidity 领域关键结构的语义评估指标），系统评估了多种代码 LLM 在仓库级 Solidity 生成任务上的表现，发现通用模型存在结构性缺陷，检索增强生成在非参数方法中表现最佳，而监督微调通过将 Solidity 特有约束内化为模型参数取得了最大提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准和指标不足以评估仓库级 Solidity 代码生成任务（需从自然语言需求合成完整合约），且通用大模型在智能合约这一高安全敏感性领域的效果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建 SolidityBench（5,470 个仓库级合约-描述对）和 SolidityScore（关注安全修饰符、合约声明、Solidity 关键词的语义指标），在零样本、思维链、上下文学习、检索增强生成和监督微调五种范式下评估 Qwen2.5-Coder、DeepSeek-Coder、CodeLlama 等模型。

Result: 通用模型在仓库级 Solidity 生成中存在系统性结构缺陷；非参数方法中检索增强生成表现最佳，上下文学习超过 2 个示例后因上下文饱和性能下降；监督微调取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 高质量领域数据结合监督微调是提升 LLM 生成智能合约可靠性的最有效策略，为仓库级 Solidity 代码生成提供了全面的基准评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in general-purpose code generation, but their effectiveness in specialized software domains remains underexplored. Solidity smart contracts represent a high-stakes domain where generated code must satisfy strict language-level, security, and software-engineering constraints. Existing benchmarks and metrics remain insufficient for repository-level Solidity generation, where models must synthesize complete contracts from natural language requirements. To address this gap, we introduce SolidityBench, a benchmark of 5,470 repository-level Solidity smart contracts paired with natural language descriptions. We also propose SolidityScore, a Solidity-aware semantic metric that emphasizes domain-critical constructs such as security modifiers, contract declarations, and Solidity-specific keywords. Using this benchmark, we evaluate representative code LLMs, including Qwen2.5-Coder, DeepSeek-Coder, and CodeLlama, across zero-shot prompting, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and supervised fine-tuning. The results show that general-purpose models exhibit systematic structural deficiencies in repository-level Solidity generation. Among non-parametric methods, retrieval-augmented generation performs best, while in-context learning degrades beyond two examples due to context saturation. Supervised fine-tuning achieves the largest improvement by internalizing Solidity-specific constraints into model parameters. Overall, our study provides a comprehensive benchmark for repository-level Solidity code generation and shows that high-quality domain data combined with supervised fine-tuning is the most effective strategy for improving the reliability of LLM-generated smart contracts.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Static Endpoints: Tool Programs as an Interface for Flexible Agentic Web Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19992)
*Mugeng Liu,Shuoqi Li,Yixuan Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolPro将LLM代理的多次工具调用意图编码为可执行工具程序，支持循环、条件、重试等复杂工作流，并通过约束构建、效应感知重放和性能策略，在MCP风格服务上实现端到端延迟降低最高53.4%、流量减少最高96.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理工具接口多为静态端点，难以表达包含循环、条件分支、连接和重试的长周期工作流，导致多步工具调用效率低下。

Method: 提出ToolPro框架：(1) 将代理工具意图表示为可执行工具程序，紧凑编码多步服务交互与显式效果类型；(2) 约束引导的程序构建；(3) 效果感知重放实现恰好一次的状态修改调用；(4) 基于性能画像的策略动态决策程序执行与逐步调用的切换。

Result: 在MCP风格服务上评估，端到端延迟降低最高53.4%，客户端流量减少最高96.1%，且网络延迟越高、工作流越复杂时收益越大。

Conclusion: ToolPro通过将工具调用序列化为可执行程序，显著提升了LLM代理在多步工具调用场景下的效率和性能，尤其适用于高延迟网络和复杂工作流环境。

Abstract: In the agentic web era, LLM-based agents increasingly invoke web services as tools, yet most interfaces remain \emph{static endpoints} that poorly express long-horizon workflows with loops, conditionals, joins, and retries. We present ToolPro, which represents an agent's tool intent as an \emph{executable tool program} that compactly encodes multi-step service interactions with explicit effect types. ToolPro combines constraint-guided program construction, effect-aware replay for exactly-once state-modifying calls, and a profile-driven policy that decides when program execution outperforms stepwise calling. We instantiate ToolPro over MCP-style services with WebAssembly sandboxing and evaluate it on diverse workflows of real-world applications. ToolPro reduces end-to-end latency by up to 53.4\% and client-side traffic by up to 96.1\%, with larger gains under higher network latency and workflow complexity.

</details>


### [185] [When Lower Privileges Suffice: Investigating Over-Privileged Tool Selection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20023)
*Kaiyue Yang,Yuyan Bu,Jingwei Yi,Yuchi Wang,Biyu Zhou,Juntao Dai,Songlin Hu,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在工具选择中的过度特权问题，提出ToolPrivBench评估框架，发现主流智能体普遍存在过度选择高特权工具的现象，瞬态故障会加剧此问题，并提出特权感知的后训练防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择研究关注安全无关的元数据偏好，忽略了特权敏感的工具选择（即在有足够低特权工具可用时，智能体仍选择高特权工具）所带来的安全风险。

Method: 构建ToolPrivBench评估框架，覆盖8个领域和5种常见风险模式，分别测量智能体的初始工具选择和瞬态故障后的升级行为。提出特权感知的后训练防御方法。

Result: 主流LLM智能体中过度特权工具选择普遍存在；瞬态故障会进一步放大该问题；通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移到最小特权工具选择；提示级控制在瞬态故障下缓解效果有限；提出的后训练防御显著减少了不必要的高特权工具使用。

Conclusion: 需要专门针对工具选择的特权限控进行安全对齐，提出的特权感知后训练防御能有效降低过度特权风险同时保持通用能力。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly select tools autonomously, their choices among tools with different privileges become safety-relevant. However, prior tool-selection studies focus on safety-agnostic metadata preferences, leaving privilege-sensitive choices underexplored. To address this gap, we study over-privileged tool selection, in which an agent selects or escalates to a higher-privilege tool despite a sufficient lower-privilege alternative. We introduce ToolPrivBench to evaluate whether agents choose higher-privilege tools despite sufficient lower-privilege alternatives, measuring both initial selection and escalation after transient tool failures. Across eight domains and five recurring risk patterns, we find that over-privileged tool selection is common among mainstream LLM agents and is further amplified by transient failures. We further find that general safety alignment does not reliably transfer to least-privilege tool choice, while prompt-level controls provide only limited mitigation under transient failures. We therefore introduce a privilege-aware post-training defense that teaches agents to prefer sufficient lower-privilege tools and escalate only when necessary. Our mitigation experiments show that this defense substantially reduces unnecessary high-privilege tool use while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [186] [The Correctness Illusion in LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20128)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试（KernelBench、TritonBench、GEAK）使用固定形状、小样本的allclose检查评估正确性的做法，提出了一种基于op-schema感知的种子化模糊测试方法，结合高精度fp64 CPU参考和每(op, dtype)绝对容差。在24个Triton和CPU替代内核（15正确+9含LLM转录错误）的受控语料上，该方法100%检测出错误内核且零精度损失；在扩展到26个ops和5类GPU上，同样100%检测出10个幻觉内核并确认16个正确内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试使用固定形状、小样本的allclose检查来评估正确性，但这种方法可能遗漏特定类型的LLM转录错误。作者希望验证这种评估方法的可靠性，并开发一种更严格的测试方法。

Method: 构建24个Triton和CPU替代内核的受控语料（15个正确控制+9个含文档化转录错误的LLM风格变体），采用op-schema感知的种子化模糊测试，使用高精度fp64 CPU参考和每(op, dtype)绝对容差进行重新评估。随后扩展至26个ops并在5类GPU（RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL）上重复实验。

Result: 种子化模糊测试在受控语料上100%检测出9个错误内核并确认15个正确内核，零精度损失。在扩展语料和5类GPU上，同样100%检测出10个幻觉内核并确认16个正确内核，且结果在所有GPU上一致。

Conclusion: 现有的固定形状allclose检查可能无法检测LLM生成的GPU内核中的转录错误，而本文提出的op-schema感知种子化模糊测试方法能有效识别这类错误。该结果针对的是LLM风格的转录错误，而非特定部署LLM的bug率。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels (KernelBench, TritonBench, GEAK) score correctness through fixed-shape, small-sample allclose-style checks. The number of inputs varies between benchmarks. The shape, dtype, and tolerance are fixed for each kernel. We test that oracle empirically. We construct a controlled corpus of 24 Triton and CPU stand-in kernels (15 correct controls and 9 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription errors) and re-evaluate it under op-schema-aware seeded fuzzing with a high-precision (fp64) CPU reference and per-(op, dtype) absolute tolerances. The seeded oracle flags 9 of 9 buggy kernels and passes 15 of 15 correct controls, at zero precision cost on controls. We extend the corpus to 26 ops (adding a flash-attention pair) and re-run the same protocol on five GPU classes (RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL). The verdicts are identical across all five GPUs: 10 of 10 illusions caught and 16 of 16 controls clean. The corpus result is about LLM-style transcription bugs that the allclose-on-one-shape oracle certifies as correct, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM. Every flagged failure replays byte-for-byte from a stored seed.

</details>


### [187] [Learning Critical Testing Literacy Through Puzzles: an Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20129)
*Niels Doorn,Bart Th. Knaack,Tanja E. J. Vos,Beatriz Marín*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了通过谜题学习批判性测试素养（CTL）的13次工作坊的经验和观察，发现谜题本身不是干预手段，完整的"解决-汇报-反思"序列才是关键，并开发了开源网络应用以支持工作坊定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试重要但教学困难，现有教学缺乏基于批判性测试者认知模型的谜题学习活动，需要探索谜题驱动教学在CTL中的有效性。

Method: 基于批判性测试者认知模型和P4TEST教学框架，开展13次工作坊（11次半结构化+2次引入工作簿和出声思维），涵盖学生、测试员、教师和小学生群体，收集行为观察、出声思维和书面反思等多模态数据。

Result: 参与者普遍感知自己在"实验"；学生趋向收敛解决方案而专业人员持续探索；情绪可通过行为观察但难以通过书面反思捕捉；出声思维揭示即时推理，书面反思引发元认知反思；核心主题为"意义建构/行动中反思"。

Conclusion: 谜题本身不是教学干预手段，完整的"解决-汇报-反思"序列才是；未来需要更刻意地设计该序列；已开发开源网络应用支持工作坊定制与分析。

Abstract: In this paper, we report our experiences and takeaways from workshops using puzzles to learn CTL.
  Background: Software testing is important yet difficult to teach. We introduced a BoK of puzzle-based learning activities to teach CTL, based on a model of critical tester's cognition, leading to the pedagogical framework P4TEST. We conducted thirteen workshops with students, testers, teachers, and primary school pupils to assess puzzle-based teaching of critical testing literacy.
  Experience: Across eleven workshops, we used a semi-structured approach, varying puzzles, materials, and timing. In two additional workshops, we introduced workbooks and think-aloud sessions to gather more data on the learning experience.
  Observations: Participants consistently perceived themselves as experimenting while solving puzzles. Students tended to converge on solutions, while professionals continued exploring. Emotions were visible in behaviour but hard to surface through written reflection alone. Think-aloud sessions revealed immediate reasoning; written reflections elicited more meta-cognitive reflection. The theme Sensemaking / reflection-in-action captured how participants framed problems, navigated dead ends, and shifted strategies.
  Reflections: Puzzles are not the intervention: the entire sequence of solving, debriefing, and reflecting is. Designing that sequence more deliberately is the work ahead. We also developed an open-source web application with built-in analytics to customise workshops.

</details>


### [188] [N-Version Programming with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20158)
*Javier Ron,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在当代AI编程智能体背景下重新审视N版本编程经典概念，通过Knight-Leveson实验的复现，评估48个智能体生成的实现版本，发现大量共因故障，但多数投票机制显著降低了故障率，表明带编程智能体的N版本编程是一种有效的工程策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当代AI编码智能体快速发展的背景下，重新审视经典N版本编程概念，验证多样性（不同智能体系统、模型和实现语言）是否能带来多样化的故障模式，从而提升软件可靠性。

Method: 采用Knight-Leveson实验中的Launch Interceptor Program Specification作为基准，使用48个智能体生成的实现版本，在共享预言机上通过1,000,000个随机化测试输入进行评估，并采用多数投票机制构建三版本单元进行故障分析。

Result: 发现了显著的共因故障模式（与Knight-Leveson原始发现一致）；多数投票机制下，三版本单元的平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，且11,844个N版本单元未观察到任何故障。

Conclusion: 提供了迄今最强有力的证据，表明带编程智能体的N版本编程是一种有用的工程策略。

Abstract: This paper revisits the classical concept on N-version programming in the setting of contemporary AI coding agents. Revisiting the seminal Knight-Leveson experiment, we study whether diversity across agent systems, models, and implementation languages creates diverse failure modes. Using the Knight-Leveson's, Launch Interceptor Program Specification, we evaluate 48 agent-generated implementations on a shared oracle and a campaign of 1,000,000 randomized test inputs. The results show substantial common-mode failure, along the findings of Knight-Leveson. Further analysis that many of those co-occuring failures can be traced to where is specification is particularly hard or ambiguous. We also demonstrate that diversity from coding agents provides practical benefit: across majority voting three-version units, the mean failure count drops from 387.44 for single versions to 130.99 for triples, and 11,844 N-version units exhibit zero observed failures. Our original results is the strongest evidence to date that N-Version Programming with coding agents is a useful engineering strategy.

</details>


### [189] [Qiskit Code Migration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20173)
*Jose Manuel Suarez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Joaquin Bogado,Alenandro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM与检索增强生成（RAG）的混合方法，利用自动生成的迁移场景分类体系作为结构化知识源，实现Qiskit代码跨版本的自动化迁移。实验表明，基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）能显著减少幻觉、提升描述质量，其中Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开发工具包（QDKs）的快速演进带来了特定形式的技术债务，损害代码可维护性并阻碍软件复用。在量子软件工程（QSE）领域，高质量训练数据稀缺和新兴框架的高波动性导致通用大语言模型（LLMs）产生不可靠或幻觉结果，迫切需要一种自动化迁移方法。

Method: 提出LLM与检索增强生成（RAG）的混合方法，核心是自动生成迁移场景分类体系作为结构化、版本特定的知识源。实现了一个自动化、可扩展的工作流，在两种检索方案（非约束性和限制性）下评估了Google Gemini Flash-2.5和OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b两种模型。

Result: 基于分类体系的RAG架构（特别是限制性检索方案）显著减少了幻觉，提升了描述质量。Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景中表现优于OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的方法能够促进技术独立性，提供鲁棒的智能助手以缓解API过时问题，确保量子算法在快速演变的生态系统中的长期可用性，并降低量子软件工程的学习曲线。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Quantum Development Kits (QDKs) introduces a specific form of technical debt that compromises code maintainability and hinders software reuse. In the specialized domain of Quantum Software Engineering (QSE), this challenge is intensified by the scarcity of high-quality training data and the high volatility of emerging frameworks, which often lead general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) to produce unreliable or hallucinated results. This paper proposes a hybrid approach integrating LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to automate the migration of Qiskit code across versions. The proposed methodology enhances the precision and reliability of migration suggestions by leveraging an automatically generated taxonomy of migration scenarios as the structured, version-specific knowledge source to guide the models. The approach is implemented through an automated, extensible workflow evaluating LLMs (Google Gemini Flash-2.5 and OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b) under different retrieval schemes (unconstrained and restrictive). Results demonstrate that the taxonomy-based RAG architecture, particularly under the restrictive scheme, significantly reduces hallucinations and improves descriptive quality, with Google Gemini Flash-2.5 showing superior performance in detecting complex refactoring scenarios. These findings confirm the potential of this data-centric methodology to foster technological independence and provide robust, intelligent assistants that mitigate API obsolescence, ensuring the long-term availability of quantum algorithms within a rapidly shifting ecosystem and flattening the learning curve within Quantum Software Engineering (QSE).

</details>


### [190] [SysML Modeling of Digital Twins for Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20230)
*Mohammad Samadi,Luís Miguel Pinho,Andrey Sadovykh,Gabriela Lucas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对可再生能源社区（REC）数字孪生工程中的异构性问题，提出了一种基于模型系统工程（MBSE）的初步工作流程，通过SysML建模并结合SAREF4ENER本体来弥补语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REC数字孪生的构建面临设备、合同和运行时数据的异构性挑战，现有方法难以有效处理这些 heterogeneity，亟需一种系统化的工程方法。

Method: 基于工业验证的REC领域模型，利用开源工具Modelio在SysML中重新表达代表性房屋子集，生成设备分类和社区组织视图两个模块定义图（BDD），并讨论SysML留下的四个语义鸿沟，提出引入SAREF4ENER本体作为参考包来弥补。

Result: 构建了SysML表达的REC设备分类图和社区组织视图，识别了SysML在语义表达上的四个不足，并展示了SAREF4ENER本体与SysML结合的初步可行性。

Conclusion: SysML与SAREF本体的结合用于智能能源数字孪生仍是一个未被充分探索的领域，本文的工作为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Renewable Energy Communities (RECs) are emerging as a key organizational model for local and global sharing of renewable generation, storage, and flexible loads. Engineering Digital Twins of RECs is made difficult by the heterogeneity of devices, contracts, and runtime data involved. In this paper, we take a first step toward a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) workflow for REC's Digital Twins. Starting from an industrially-validated REC domain model, we re-express a representative house subset in SysML using the open-source Modelio tool, yielding two Block Definition Diagrams - a device taxonomy and a community organizational view. We then discuss four semantic gaps that plain SysML leaves open and sketch how the SAREF4ENER ontology could be imported as a reference package to close them. Combining SysML with SAREF-based semantics for smart-energy Digital Twins remains largely unexplored, and we position this paper as a first step along that line.

</details>


### [191] [Phoenix: Safe GitHub Issue Resolution via Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20243)
*Kipngeno Koech,Muhammad Adam,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Joao Barros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Phoenix是一个多智能体LLM系统，通过6个专业化智能体和7层安全控制，实现从问题分类到PR创建的GitHub问题自动解析。在SWE-bench Lite子集上oracle解决率达75%，在42个真实问题上保持100%正确性，但约一半PR存在路径定位错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在自动化解决GitHub问题时缺乏安全控制和生产环境部署的可靠性保障，且缺乏从问题分类到PR创建的端到端自动化方案。

Method: Phoenix采用6个专业化智能体（Planner、Reproducer、Coder、Tester、Failure Analyst、PR Agent），由基于标签的GitHub webhook状态机协调，结合7层安全控制和基线测试评估策略。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite 24实例子集上oracle解决率75%，无通过回归；在42个真实问题上正确性保持率100%，平均耗时122秒；约一半PR修复定位准确。

Conclusion: Phoenix展示了多智能体LLM系统在自动化代码修复中的潜力，但Planner智能体存在代码路径定位限制，需通过检索增强解决；同时部署中的安全机制（WAF过滤、令牌过期、权限边界、不稳定CI）是保障可靠性的关键。

Abstract: We present Phoenix, a multi-agent LLM system that resolves GitHub issues from triage through pull-request creation, combining seven layered safety controls with a baseline-aware test evaluation strategy. Phoenix decomposes the work across six specialized agents. Planner, reproducer, coder, tester, failure analyst and Pull Request (PR) agent, all coordinated by a label-based GitHub webhook state machine. Every change is checked against a baseline test run before a pull request is opened. On a 24-instance slice of SWE-bench Lite. run on the production webhook path, Phoenix oracle-resolves 75% of instances with no pass-to-pass regressions on successful runs; this curated slice is not directly comparable to full-split leaderboard results, and we discuss the limits of the comparison. A complementary pilot on 42 real issues across 14 repositories yields 100% correctness preservation (CP; mean 122s on the hard tier). Manual inspection shows that about half of the resulting pull requests are well-targeted fixes. The other half place code at incorrect paths, a planner localization limitation we are addressing with retrieval. We also report the deployment failure modes (WAF filtering, token expiry, permission boundaries, flaky CI) that motivated each safety mechanism.

</details>


### [192] [Token-Operations-Oriented Inference Optimization Techniques for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20295)
*Shiguo Lian,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Wen Liu,Minjie Hua,Yutong Liu,Jiangze Yan,Xin Wang,Cong Wang,Yilin Zhang,Yi Shen,Jieyun Huang,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Ping Chen,Xinyu Yang,Kaikai Zhao,Yao Zhao,Xinggang Wang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Junping Du,Tao Chen,Xiang Gao,Qinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文围绕面向Token的大模型推理优化技术，首次提出四层技术架构（多模型融合、模型优化、计算-模型融合、计算-网络-模型融合），系统综述了各层关键技术及产业现状，分析了在实际业务场景中的应用价值，旨在降低Token生产成本、提升服务效率、保障供给稳定性，推动大模型服务从“可用”迈向“可运营”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务需要可扩展、低成本、高稳定性的推理优化技术支撑，现有研究缺乏系统性的技术架构梳理，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出面向Token的推理优化四层技术架构：多模型融合、模型优化、计算-模型融合、计算-网络-模型融合，并逐层综述关键技术和产业现状。

Result: 系统梳理了各层关键技术，分析了相关技术在真实业务场景中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 该四层架构为降低Token生产成本、提升服务效率、保障供给稳定性提供了实践路径，推动大模型服务从可用到可运营的转变。

Abstract: Large model inference optimization serves as a key foundation for supporting the scalable, low-cost, and highly stable operation of large model services. Centered on token-oriented inference optimization technology, this paper proposes for the first time a four-layer technical architecture consisting of Multi-model Fusion, Model Optimization, Compute-Model Fusion, and Compute-Network-Model Fusion. It systematically reviews the key technologies and current industry status across these four levels and analyzes the application value of related technologies in real-world business scenarios. This paper provides a practical technical path for reducing token production costs, improving token service efficiency, ensuring the stability of token supply, and driving the transition of large model services from being merely callable to being operable.

</details>


### [193] [A Model-Driven Approach for Developing Families of Reinforcement Learning Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20324)
*Xiaoran Liu,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型驱动的方法，通过混合遗传算法（全局搜索+启发式局部搜索）结合模型转换技术，自动化生成强化学习训练所需的环境变体家族，并以野火缓解场景和课程学习为例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL训练环境家族的开发过程依赖人工，劳动密集且易出错，可扩展性差，亟需一种自动化的方法来生成多组相似但略有不同的训练环境变体。

Method: 提出模型驱动方法，采用混合遗传算法（种群全局搜索+启发式局部搜索），将变异和约束表达为模型转换，通过先进的模型转换引擎将搜索过程操作化，并开发了原型工具。

Result: 在野火缓解场景和课程学习范式中验证了方法的正确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型驱动方法能够自动化、可扩展地生成RL训练环境家族，减轻人工开发负担，尤其适用于依赖环境家族的课程学习等学习范式。

Abstract: Virtual training environments are software-intensive systems in which reinforcement learning (RL) agents learn, adapt, and demonstrate meaningful behavior. Virtual training environments offer a safe and cost-efficient alternative to training agents in real-world settings. However, to converge, most realistic RL problems require training in multiple, mostly similar but slightly different environments - i.e., families of environment variants. The typical development process of environment families is a labor-intensive and error-prone manual endeavor that does not scale well. To alleviate these issues, in this paper, we propose a model-driven approach for developing families of RL training environments. To obtain the family of environments, we develop an approach and prototype tool. In our approach, a hybrid genetic algorithm - a combination of population-based global search and heuristic local search - generates environment families. Mutations and constraints are expressed as model transformations and are operationalized into a search process by a state-of-the-art model transformation engine. We demonstrate the soundness of our approach in a wildfire mitigation scenario and curriculum learning - a particular learning paradigm that relies on environment families.

</details>


### [194] [AutoPass: Evidence-Guided LLM Agents for Compiler Performance Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20373)
*Zepeng Li,Jie Ren,Zhanyong Tang,Jie Zheng,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoPass是一个基于多智能体LLM的编译器性能调优框架，通过开放编译器内部状态给LLM，利用编译和运行时证据迭代优化编译配置，在无需训练的情况下在x86-64和ARM64上分别实现1.043x和1.117x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码编译任务中展现出潜力，但将其应用于运行时性能调优面临微架构效应复杂和运行时测量噪声大的挑战。现有自动调优方案将编译器视为黑盒，无法充分利用编译器内部信息来指导优化决策。

Method: 提出AutoPass，一个多智能体框架：1) 向LLM开放编译器内部状态，允许查询编译器优化状态和分析中间表示(IR)；2) 利用编译和运行时证据指导LLM生成优化决策；3) 通过迭代搜索过程，使用运行时反馈诊断性能回归并指导延迟改进编辑；4) 采用推理-only、无需训练或微调的方式运行。

Result: 在LLVM编译器上实现，并在x86-64和ARM64平台评估。AutoPass超越专家调优启发式方法和经典自动调优方法，在x86-64上相比LLVM -O3实现1.043x几何平均加速，在ARM64上实现1.117x几何平均加速。

Conclusion: AutoPass证明了通过向LLM开放编译器内部状态并利用运行时反馈，可以有效解决编译器性能调优难题，且无需训练或微调即可适用于新基准和平台，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for code compilation tasks, but applying them to runtime performance tuning is difficult due to complex microarchitectural effects and noisy runtime measurements. We present AutoPass, a multi-agent framework for compiler performance tuning that uses compiler and runtime evidence to guide LLM-generated optimization decisions. Rather than treating the compiler as a black box like prior auto-tuning schemes, AutoPass opens up the compiler to the LLM, enabling it to query compiler-internal optimization states and analyze the intermediate representation to orchestrate compiler options. The search process iteratively refines optimization configurations using measured runtime feedback to diagnose regressions and guide latency-improving edits. AutoPass operates in an inference-only, training-free setting and requires no offline training or task-specific fine-tuning, making it readily applicable to new benchmarks and platforms. We implement AutoPass on the LLVM compiler and evaluate it on server-grade x86-64 and embedded ARM64 systems. AutoPass outperforms expert-tuned heuristics and classical autotuning methods, achieving geometric-mean speedups of 1.043x and 1.117x over LLVM -O3 on x86-64 and ARM64, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [Probe-and-Refine Tuning of Repository Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20512)
*Asa Shepard,Jeannie Albrecht*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 probe-and-refine tuning 方法，通过合成 bug-fix 探针迭代优化仓库的 AGENTS.md 指导文件，在 SWE-bench Verified 上将编码代理的修复率从 28.3%（静态知识库）和 25.5%（无指导基线）提升至 33.0%，且提升源于覆盖率增加而非精度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理需要仓库级操作知识（如子系统结构、测试运行方式、历史失败工作流），这些信息不存在于代码本身。工程师通常维护 AGENTS.md 文件提供上下文，但 LLM 生成的指导是否有效存在争议，近期研究结论相互矛盾。

Method: 提出 probe-and-refine tuning：使用合成 bug-fix 探针，通过单次 LLM 调用迭代诊断并修补仓库指导文件，期间不涉及代理循环或工具使用。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 在 200 步下平均修复率 33.0%，优于静态知识库基线 28.3% 和无指导基线 25.5%（p<0.001）。改进来自覆盖率提升 14.5pp，而每补丁精度保持约 59% 不变（p=0.119）。跨模型实验显示当模型无法生成足够诊断性输出时调优效果退化。

Conclusion: 指导文件的生产方式是决定编码代理性能的关键变量；probe-and-refine 通过提升覆盖范围而非精度来改善代理性能，且指导能帮助代理有效利用更大的步骤预算。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents need higher-level operational knowledge about a repository (which files house which subsystems, how to run the test suite, which workflows have historically led to wrong fixes) that does not exist in the code itself. Engineers typically maintain AGENTS.md files to supply this context as instructions for coding agents, but whether they help is contested: recent studies disagree on whether LLM-generated guidance improves or harms agent performance. In this paper we show that how the guidance is produced is the decisive variable, and introduce probe-and-refine tuning: a procedure that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively diagnose and patch a repository's guidance file through single-shot LLM calls, with no agent loop or tool use during tuning. On SWE-bench Verified across four independent trials with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B at 200 steps, probe-and-refine achieves 33.0% mean resolve rate vs. 28.3% for the static knowledge base used to initialize it and 25.5% for an unguided baseline (p < 0.001 for both probe-and-refine contrasts). The improvement comes from coverage rather than precision: refined guidance produces evaluable patches for 14.5 percentage points (pp) more instances while per-patch precision remains statistically constant (~59%, p = 0.119), showing that improved guidance helps agents reach the correct file rather than improving the quality of the changes they make. Further, a step-budget experiment shows that guidance is what lets the agent use a larger step budget productively, and a cross-model experiment with NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B finds that the tuning loop degrades when the model cannot generate sufficiently diagnostic output, though per-patch precision remains constant even then.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP是一个面向远程内存的算子优化框架，通过引入传输轮次感知的缓存策略，在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接定制内存分区方案，相比传统方案减少高达97%的传输轮次和48%的算子运行时间，端到端TPC-H和TPC-DS查询性能分别提升22.7%和26.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程内存和分离式内存层级扩展了分析数据库引擎的有效内存容量，但重塑了内存溢出查询处理的成本结构。传统算子分析和缓存分配启发式方法主要面向磁盘溢出（最小化总I/O量），在远程内存场景下可能触发过多的传输轮次，导致次优性能。

Method: 将传输轮次数引入延迟成本模型，推导算子特定的缓存分区策略，在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接三种算子实例化该框架。

Result: 在双节点计算-存储测试平台上，REMOP在溢出密集的微基准测试中减少高达97%的传输轮次和48%的算子运行时间；端到端TPC-H和TPC-DS溢出查询平均运行时间分别降低22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: 面向远程内存的算子优化需要引入传输轮次感知的缓存策略，REMOP通过算子内内存策略优化，可以在严格内存预算下显著提升内存溢出执行效率。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [197] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL是一种扩展SQL的决策查询语言，通过CREATE CANDIDATES定义候选空间、DECIDE声明决策变量/约束/目标，支持子集选择、分配、调度等决策场景，并支持不确定性优化、模型评分和限时求解，用户只需声明优化目标而由引擎自动求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQL无法表达决策问题（如从关系数据中选择最优方案），用户需要手动编写求解逻辑，缺乏声明式的优化查询能力。DeQL旨在填补这一空白，让用户以SQL风格声明式地表达决策问题。

Method: 在SQL中引入两个核心构造：CREATE CANDIDATES（从关系数据定义候选选项空间）和DECIDE（声明决策变量、命名约束和优化目标）。设计遵循SQL原则：用户声明优化什么，引擎决定如何求解；每个查询输入输出均为关系；问题结构对引擎可见。

Result: 提出了DeQL语言的完整规范，包括设计原则、语法、形式化文法、执行模型，并通过子集选择、分配、调度、多层级聚合决策等示例验证，还扩展了不确定性优化、内联模型评分、时间和质量限界求解。

Conclusion: DeQL作为SQL的声明式扩展，能够表达各类决策优化问题，当前版本固定了核心构造，后续版本将在其基础上发展，语言仍在积极开发中。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [198] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对向量数据库在细粒度访问控制（FGAC）方面的不足，提出了策略感知向量搜索的愿景，形式化了FGAC策略模型和执行问题，比较了不同策略执行方法，并指出了未来研究的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库在RAG和AI管线等安全敏感场景中广泛使用，但现有系统不支持细粒度访问控制（FGAC），无法确保数据访问符合用户特定策略。与传统关系数据库不同，向量数据库结合了结构化和非结构化属性，提供语义化的近似查询结果，这使FGAC实现复杂化。

Method: 形式化定义了向量数据库中FGAC策略模型和执行问题；比较了多种策略执行策略；通过初步实验评估了不同方法的性能表现。

Result: 指出了FGAC执行与高ANN召回率、低查询延迟之间存在固有权衡；初步发现不同执行策略在正确性和性能上各有优劣。

Conclusion: 策略感知向量搜索是一个重要但尚未充分研究的课题，存在多个开放挑战，包括如何在保证FGAC正确性的同时维持高效的近似最近邻搜索性能。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [199] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种查询感知的路由框架，通过轻量级ML模型预测不同过滤ANN方法在查询上的召回率，并结合离线基准表选择最佳召回-QPS权衡的方法，在五个未见验证集上实现了最先进的召回与QPS平衡，且延迟开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤ANN方法在不同数据集、谓词类型乃至同一数据集的不同查询上表现差异显著，没有单一方法能全面占优，因此需要一种能够自适应选择最优方法的机制。

Method: 提出查询感知路由框架：1) 轻量级ML模型预测各候选方法在查询上的召回率；2) 路由模块查询离线基准表（映射方法与参数设置到实测召回率和QPS），选择召回-QPS权衡最佳的方法。通过消融研究将22个候选特征缩减至3个，采用回归而非分类作为预测目标以提高精度。

Result: 在6个真实数据集上训练，在5个未见验证集上评估，路由器的召回与QPS平衡优于所有现有过滤ANN基线方法，且延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 查询感知路由框架有效解决了过滤ANN方法选择问题，实现了跨数据集和查询的自适应最优性能，证明了轻量级ML模型结合离线基准表是一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [200] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道通信范式，将用户态UDP用于高性能数据路径、内核TCP用于可靠控制路径，以解决高速网络下数据库系统因内核TCP堆栈导致的CPU开销瓶颈问题，在200 Gbit/s分布式洗牌和百万级消息复制键值存储中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高速云网络中，传统内核TCP堆栈的CPU开销成为数据库性能瓶颈，而直接替换为用户态UDP需要重新实现可靠性、有序性等关键保证，亟需一种兼顾高性能与可靠性的网络-数据库协同设计方案。

Method: 提出双通道通信范式，将数据库通信分离为两条通道：使用用户态UDP构建高性能数据路径处理延迟和带宽敏感的操作，使用内核TCP构建可靠控制路径处理协调和恢复。利用现代NIC能力的同时保留TCP的可靠性保证。

Result: 在分布式洗牌场景中，仅用三个CPU核心即可饱和200 Gbit/s带宽；在复制键值存储场景中，每秒可处理数百万条消息。

Conclusion: 数据库系统应将网络与数据库操作协同设计而非将网络视为黑盒，双通道范式通过UDP/TCP混合设计在保留可靠性的同时显著提升性能，验证了该范式的有效性和简洁性。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [201] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDB框架，将数据库配置调优转变为基于运行时反馈的自优化重配置过程，支持DBMS和OS级联合调优，在MySQL和PostgreSQL上相比最强基线平均性能提升118.1%，达成最佳时间缩短22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调优器将调优视为对DBMS knob值的迭代搜索，导致执行成本高、过早缩小配置空间，且无法满足实际需求：诊断运行时瓶颈、探索OS级重配置机会、稳健执行变更以及从历史任务中学习。

Method: 提出AgenticDB智能体框架，实现上下文驱动的交互机制：与目标数据库环境交互，提出DBMS和OS级变更，在安全约束下执行变更，监控工作负载性能和运行时状态，利用执行反馈指导后续决策，支持跨任务经验积累。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上使用YCSB、Sysbench和TPC-H工作负载进行实验，AgenticDB在所有工作负载上取得最佳最终性能，平均提升最强基线118.1%，aggregate time-to-best减少22.6%。OS级动作空间、稳健执行生命周期和记忆增强规划均贡献显著。

Conclusion: AgenticDB将数据库调优转变为基于运行时反馈的自优化重配置过程，通过DBMS和OS级联合探索、安全约束执行和经验积累，实现了更高效且实用的数据库重配置方案。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


### [202] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对云存储缓存中按GET请求和出站字节计费（而非延迟）的实际成本模型，提出了面向美元成本最优的离线策略基准。通过区间线性规划实现了均匀大小页缓存的精确最优解，并扩展了变长页的成本感知流界。实验揭示了异构性遗憾定律、竞争边界以及跨域阈值s*，为实际部署提供了成本感知缓存的决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缓存优化以缺失率为目标，但在云存储计费模式下（按GET请求和出站流量计费），缺失率最小化不等同于成本最小化——某些稀有但昂贵的对象可能比频繁但便宜的对象多花费数千倍。缺乏面向实际云价格的美元最优离线策略基准，无法衡量现有启发式算法与理论最优之间的差距。

Method: 提出积分区间线性规划实现均匀大小页缓存的精确离线美元最优解；对于NP难的变长页情形，扩展基于流的离线界（cost-FOO）至约4%的紧致度。在真实云价格（S3、GCS）和真实负载（memcache、CDN）上进行了系统评估。

Result: (i)异构性遗憾定律：LRU的美元遗憾随缺失成本分散度上升（Spearman 0.87），而成本感知的GreedyDual将其降低约一个数量级；(ii)竞争边界：GreedyDual的残差遗憾在预算恰好覆盖昂贵工作集时趋近于零，否则为开放切片；(iii)跨域阈值 s* = GET_fee/egress_rate（S3约4 KB，GCS约330 B），可预测哪些部署需要成本感知缓存。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个面向美元成本的离线最优基准，揭示了传统LRU在云计费模型下的显著低效，并给出了成本感知缓存的实用指导（跨域阈值s*）。研究成果可复现，启发式方法和理论界基于已有工作并予以致谢。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching. On real memcache and CDN traces the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Simulating Robotic Locomotion in Sand: Resistive Force Theory in an Open-Source Physics Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19504)
*Ryan Walker Brown,Laura K. Treers,Kathryn A. Daltorio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒介质阻力理论(3D RFT)，用于模拟机器人在沙地上的行走。实验验证表明，该方法能在20%误差范围内预测六足机器人的行走距离和脚部下陷深度，为机器人穿越颗粒介质的仿真提供了高效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阻力理论(RFT)可近似计算沙地 locomotion 的地面反作用力，但缺乏在3D物理引擎中的实现，限制了机器人仿真中颗粒介质行走的研究。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理引擎中集成3D颗粒阻力理论(3D RFT)，结合标准动力学计算，仿真12自由度六足机器人在沙地上的行走。

Result: 仿真与实验对比显示，行走距离和脚部下陷深度的预测误差在20%以内，且能正确反映末端执行器形状、速度和负载等关键趋势。

Conclusion: 尽管RFT存在固有近似，该开源工具为开发和改进穿越颗粒介质基底的机器人设计提供了有效平台。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Resistive Force Theory (RFT) enable approximation of ground reaction forces for locomotion in sand without the computational expense of modeling interactions with individual grains. However, these tools have been absent in 3D physics engines commonly used for robot simulation. We explore if resistive force approximations are sufficient, when integrated with standard dynamics calculations, to provide a stable substrate for a freely walking robot. To determine this, we implement 3D Granular Resistive Force Theory (3D RFT) in a physics simulation engine, MuJoCo. We verify simulations in multiple scenarios to demonstrate that key trends due to end effector shape, speed, and loading are preserved. Our implementation predicts walking distance and foot sinkage of a 12-Degree of Freedom hexapod robot within 20\% of experiments in sand. While RFT has inherent approximations, the open source tool described here has potential to help develop new and improved robot designs to traverse granular media substrates.

</details>


### [204] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Physical Atari系统，包括Robotroller机械臂（操控Atari CX40+控制器）、Atari Devbox（渲染游戏画面和奖励信号）、摄像头和台式电脑，总成本低于1000美元。该系统可连续数周无故障运行强化学习实验，验证了算法可直接在机器人上学习，并发现学习与部署间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能，强调了设备上自适应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究多基于模拟环境，缺乏一个稳健、低成本、可复现的物理平台来研究真实世界中的强化学习算法，特别是分布偏移对策略性能的影响。

Method: 设计Robotroller机械臂（所有运动通过轴承完成以减少磨损），配合高频伺服状态监测软件（干预以限制应力），结合Atari Devbox渲染ALE游戏画面和奖励信号，构成完整的Physical Atari系统。

Result: 系统可在低于1000美元成本下建造，连续数周无机械故障运行RL实验；验证了RL算法可直接在机器人上学习；发现学习与部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: Physical Atari为物理世界RL研究提供了稳健且可访问的平台；设备上自适应对于在机器人上获得强性能至关重要。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [205] [Proprioceptive Invariant State Estimation for Humanoid Robots on Non-Inertial Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19512)
*Falak Mandali,Zijian He,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）的方法，仅利用本体感觉传感实现非惯性地面上双足机器人的实时状态估计，在摇摆/俯仰地面上站立和蹲起实验中收敛速度提升96%，位置误差降低80%，在旋转地面上行走实验中初始误差1米时平均估计误差小于9厘米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖外部传感器或地面运动测量，无法在非惯性地面（如移动平台、船舶、飞行器等）上仅靠本体感觉实现机器人状态精确估计，需要一种全自主、不依赖外部感知的估计方案。

Method: 采用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）框架，利用足部IMU和支撑脚运动学约束，建立符合右不变测量模型的估计器，在过程模型和测量模型中显式处理地面引起的非线性，并进行了可观测性分析。

Result: 在Digit双足机器人上，摇摆/俯仰地面站立蹲起实验中，收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%；单轴旋转地面行走实验中，初始误差1米时平均估计误差小于9厘米。

Conclusion: 所提全本体感觉InEKF方法能有效估计非惯性地面上机器人的相对基座位置和速度，具有更快的收敛速度和更高的估计精度，适用于缺乏外部定位信息的移动平台场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an invariant extended Kalman filtering (InEKF) approach for real-time state estimation of humanoid robots operating on non-inertial ground using only onboard proprioceptive sensing. The proposed approach estimates the robot's base position and velocity relative to the moving ground frame without requiring direct measurements of ground motion or externally mounted sensors. By exploiting kinematic constraints at the stance foot through foot-mounted IMUs, the filter accounts for ground-induced nonlinearities in the process and measurement models while remaining fully proprioceptive. The estimator is formulated to admit a right-invariant measurement model, enabling favorable error dynamics under large initial uncertainties. Observability analysis establishes conditions under which the robot's relative base position and velocity are observable with respect to the non-inertial ground frame. Experiments with the Digit humanoid robot standing and squatting atop a swaying and pitching ground showcase a 96% speedup in convergence rate and an 80% reduction in position estimate errors over existing InEKFs. Walking experiments on a uni-axially rotating ground achieve an average estimation error of less than 9 cm for an initial error of up to 1 m.

</details>


### [206] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WorkBenchMark，一个基于乐高得宝的机器人装配基准测试，包含400个任务和四个复杂度层级，并提出了基于装配即拆卸的规划基线方案，该方案在所有层级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配需要结合底层操作与任务级符号推理，而当前的端到端学习方法尚无法可靠解决这一问题，因此需要专门的基准测试来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了包含400个任务和四个复杂度层级的基准测试，提出了基于装配即拆卸的开放式词汇感知规划基线方案。

Result: 所提出的规划基线方案在所有复杂度层级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。

Conclusion: 该基准测试、仿真环境和基线实现将开源发布，以支持机器人装配社区的研究。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [207] [pdSTL: Probabilistic Differentiable Signal Temporal Logic for Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19561)
*Bennett Dogbey,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出pdSTL框架，将概率语义与可微鲁棒性结合，实现不确定环境下带形式化概率保证的时序逻辑约束优化，在仿真和真实四旋翼飞行实验中优于确定性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL扩展要么缺乏可微性（无法用于梯度优化），要么忽略信念空间不确定性（无法处理随机动力学和感知噪声），缺乏同时具备概率语义和可微鲁棒性的框架。

Method: 提出pdSTL：(1) 采用区间值概率语义计算保守满足界，并通过STL语法树组合传播；(2) 将时序鲁棒性评估建模为类似LSTM的循环展开结构，实现线性时间可微监控。

Result: 在仿真避障、换道和真实Crazyflie四旋翼飞行实验中，pdSTL在保持安全裕度方面显著优于确定性的可微STL，同时实现高效优化并具备形式化概率保证。

Conclusion: pdSTL是首个统一概率语义与可微鲁棒性的STL框架，能够在不确定环境下为机器人轨迹优化提供形式化安全保证，且计算效率适合端到端优化。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in uncertain environments must satisfy complex temporal and safety specifications despite stochastic dynamics and sensing noise. While Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers robustness measures for gradient-based optimization, existing extensions either lack differentiability or ignore belief-space uncertainty. We introduce pdSTL (probabilistic differentiable Signal Temporal Logic), a framework that unifies probabilistic semantics with differentiable robustness over belief trajectories. pdSTL employs interval-valued probabilistic semantics to compute conservative satisfaction bounds, propagated compositionally through the STL syntax tree. We formulate the temporal robustness evaluation as a recurrent, LSTM-style unfolding of STL operators, enabling linear-time, differentiable monitoring suitable for end-to-end trajectory optimization. We validate pdSTL on simulated obstacle avoidance, lane-change maneuvers, and real-world Crazyflie quadcopter flight experiments under aerodynamic disturbances. Results demonstrate that pdSTL achieves efficient optimization with formal probabilistic guarantees, significantly outperforming deterministic differentiable STL in maintaining safety margins under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [208] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对3D场景图（3DSGs）进行了统一的批判性综述，形式化了其定义与建模选择，梳理了从原始传感器数据构建的流程，并系统分析了在下游应用中的评估策略，指出了该领域碎片化问题及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图（3DSGs）在空间AI中具有强大的表达能力，但该领域存在碎片化问题：不同社区采用不同的表述、构建流程和评估协议，导致难以比较方法、识别共同假设和评估真实部署中的挑战。因此，需要一份统一的、批判性的综述来整合现有研究。

Method: 首先形式化3DSGs的共同定义，分析建模选择（节点/边属性、层次结构、动态场景表示、可通行性扩展）；然后梳理从原始传感器数据构建3DSGs的常见术语、惯例和技术；最后考察下游应用及评估策略，包括内在图质量和任务级性能。

Result: 提供了一份统一的3DSG综述，明确了现有建模选择与构建管线，系统总结了评估策略，并指出了开放挑战与未来方向。同时提供了配套网站（https://3dscenegraphs.com/）来组织和扩展综述内容。

Conclusion: 3DSGs领域的碎片化需要通过统一形式化和评估标准来解决；现有方法在动态场景表示、层次化结构和可通行性建模等方面仍存在挑战；未来研究应关注跨社区标准化和真实世界鲁棒部署。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [209] [Safe, Real-Time Active Model Discrimination and Fault Diagnosis for Nonlinear Systems via Differentiable Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19590)
*Xinpei Ni,Melkior Ornik,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于不确定连续时间非线性系统的安全、实时主动故障诊断与模型区分算法。该算法在有限时域内鲁棒地满足状态-输入安全约束，并驱动系统产生与最多一个候选模型一致的测量值，实现确定性诊断。通过区间过近似可达集和可微目标函数，使用JAX和可微可达性原语在线高效求解，在多种机器人平台上实现了50ms内的可靠诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动故障诊断方法在处理不确定连续时间非线性系统时，面临实时性不足、安全约束难以保证以及诊断可靠性有限等挑战。本文旨在开发一种既能提供形式化安全保障，又能在实时条件下高效执行的主动故障诊断算法。

Method: 该方法将故障诊断表述为一个输出反馈、时变策略优化问题。核心步骤包括：(1) 使用区间过近似对可达状态集和输出集进行保守估计；(2) 通过可微目标函数量化不同模型可达输出集之间的重叠程度，将可诊断性编码为优化目标；(3) 利用JAX和可微可达性原语实现梯度优化在线求解。

Result: 在多种高维非线性机器人系统（包括四旋翼飞行器、战斗机模型、差速驱动机器人和四足导航）的传感器与执行器故障诊断（最多11种故障模式）中，该方法在50ms内实现了可靠的模型区分，在区分成功率和速度上均优于基线方法，并提供了形式化安全保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法成功将主动故障诊断的鲁棒性、安全性和实时性统一起来，通过在可达性分析框架中嵌入可微优化，实现了高效、安全的在线故障诊断，适用于对安全性要求苛刻的机器人系统。

Abstract: We present a safe, real-time algorithm for active fault diagnosis and model discrimination for uncertain continuous-time nonlinear systems with process and measurement disturbances. Given a finite set of candidate models representing nominal and faulty modes, including actuator and sensor faults, we formulate an output-feedback, time-varying policy optimization problem that (i) robustly enforces state-input safety constraints over a finite horizon and (ii) drives the system to produce sampled measurements consistent with at most one model, enabling deterministic diagnosis. To solve this problem in real time, we develop a tractable approximation using interval over-approximations of reachable state and output sets, and encode diagnosability via a differentiable objective that penalizes overlap between the reachable output sets of possible models. The resulting optimization is solved efficiently online with gradient-based methods using JAX and differentiable reachability primitives. We evaluate our method on sensor and actuator fault diagnosis (up to 11 fault modes) in several high-dimensional nonlinear robotic systems, including a simulated quadrotor and fighter-jet model, a hardware differential-drive robot, and quadrupedal navigation. Across these case studies, our approach achieves reliable model discrimination in under 50 ms, outperforming baselines in discrimination success rate and speed while providing formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [210] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散策略的视觉伺服方法，利用归一化标签角点坐标作为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度，并采用在线训练范式持续扩展训练数据多样性，在仿真中达到近100%成功率，物理实验中达到93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归式视觉伺服受限于噪声敏感的单步映射和分布偏移下的误差累积，导致轨迹抖动。扩散策略虽能通过预测动作序列保持时间一致性和鲁棒性，但面临静态数据集训练的泛化局限。

Method: 基于扩散策略，以归一化图像标签角点坐标为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度；采用在线训练范式，通过交互经验收集持续扩展训练数据多样性，增强模型泛化能力。

Result: 仿真实验成功率近100%，物理实验成功率93%。将扩散模块集成到现有视觉伺服网络中均能提升性能，验证了该策略的通用性。

Conclusion: 提出的扩散驱动视觉伺服方法具有广泛适用性，能显著提升多种视觉伺服系统性能，超越了本文具体架构的限制。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [211] [Comparative Study on Agility, Efficiency, and Impact Absorption of Bipedal Robots with Active Toes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19699)
*Joong-Gil Kim,Wontae Ye,Geunwoo Cho,Seong-Ho Yun,Se-Hyoung Cho,Yong-Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种14自由度仿人足部双足机器人，通过高保真仿真环境验证了主动脚趾在行走效率、敏捷性和冲击吸收方面的显著优势：相比无脚趾配置，CoT降低17.5%，足跟着地冲击力降低5.0%，路径偏差减少25.0%-34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类脚趾对行走的高效性、敏捷性和冲击吸收至关重要，但现有机器人未能充分复现人类脚趾特性，也缺乏对其益处的严格量化验证。

Method: 设计14自由度双足机器人以模拟人类脚趾的轻量化、高扭矩、鲁棒特性；构建考虑耦合传动和精确功耗的高保真仿真训练环境；采用最小化RL奖励函数对有无主动脚趾两种配置进行相同流程的训练与对比。

Result: 在1.33m/s行走速度下，带脚趾配置相比无脚趾配置：CoT降低17.5%，足跟着地地面反作用力降低5.0%；敏捷性测试中平均路径偏差减少25.0%，最大路径偏差减少34.0%。

Conclusion: 主动脚趾可显著提升双足机器人的行走经济性、冲击缓冲能力和敏捷性，验证了仿人脚趾设计在足式机器人中的关键价值。

Abstract: Human legs exhibit high efficiency, agility, and impact absorption, with toes playing a crucial role in these capabilities. While many attempts have been made to implement human-like toes in robots, they have not fully replicated human characteristics nor rigorously validated their benefits. We propose a 14-DOF biped robot emulating human toes' lightweight, high-torque, robust nature. To quantitatively analyze the effectiveness of the active toes in terms of agility, efficiency, and impact absorption, we developed a high-fidelity simulation training environment that reflects actual actuators with coupled transmissions and accurate power consumption. To ensure a fair comparison between configurations with and without active toes, we designed a minimal RL reward function and applied an identical training procedure to both. The simulation results indicate that, at 1.33 m/s walking, the toe-equipped robot reduced CoT by 17.5% and heel-strike GRF by 5.0% compared with the toe-ablation configuration. On the agility test, average and maximum path deviation decreased by 25.0% and 34.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [212] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），通过让具身编码智能体在目标任务到来前进行自主玩耍式学习，将成功的策略代码提炼为可复用的技能库，在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces等基准上显著提升了下游任务表现，且无需微调即可迁移到其他Code-as-Policy系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身机器人系统虽然能够编写代码策略并基于反馈进行迭代修正，但它们本质上是任务驱动的——只有在收到明确指令后才会获取可复用技能。本文旨在探索如何让机器人在没有明确任务指令的情况下，通过自主玩耍式学习主动积累可复用技能，从而提升后续下游任务的执行能力。

Method: 提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），在玩耍阶段自主提出新颖但可学习的探索任务，规划并执行机器人代码策略，验证中间进度，诊断失败原因，利用密集的步骤级反馈进行重试，并将成功执行的策略提炼为持久的代码技能库。在测试时，智能体从冻结的技能库中检索并复用相关技能来辅助解决新任务。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上，玩耍式学习的技能相比无玩耍和随机玩耍基线分别带来20.6和17.0个百分点的提升；学到的技能可直接插入其他Code-as-Policy智能体，在RoboSuite和真实世界迁移中分别提升8.9和8.8个百分点，无需微调底层模型。

Conclusion: 自主玩耍式技能学习可以有效地为具身智能体积累可复用技能，显著提升下游任务性能，并且学到的技能具有良好的可迁移性和即插即用特性，为无需微调即可提升机器人系统能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively. Moreover, the learned skills can be plugged into other inference-time Code-as-Policy agents by simply retrieving them into the context, improving RoboSuite and real-world transfer by 8.9 and 8.8 points, respectively, without finetuning the underlying model.

</details>


### [213] [A Differentiable Composite Approximation Framework for Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Maneuvering Modeling from Sea-Trial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19711)
*Aobo Wang,Aifei Xia,Zihao Wang,Lizhu Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微复合逼近方法，将多项式基分量与数据自适应基分量联合校准，并结合转向运动电流估计补偿机制，用于基于实船数据的AUV操纵建模，在海试数据上优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从基函数视角看，传统操纵模型使用预定义多项式基，而数据驱动模型使用数据自适应基，缺乏将两者协同优化的方法。且海试数据中海洋电流干扰严重影响建模精度，需要有效的电流估计与补偿机制。

Method: 提出可微复合逼近框架：1) 将多项式基与神经网络基作为单一预测器的可微部分进行联合梯度校准；2) 敏感度感知机制约束多项式更新范围，神经网络残差捕获非线性偏差；3) 基于转向运动的电流估计与补偿程序，构建电流补偿后的学习目标。

Result: 在7米长AUV多操纵工况海试数据上验证，所提方法在递归轨迹和速度预测上均优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线方法。

Conclusion: 该可微复合逼近框架有效结合了传统模型的结构先验与数据驱动模型的适应性，为基于实船数据的AUV操纵建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Field-based modeling from onboard measurements can produce autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) maneuvering models that reflect real operating characteristics. From an approximation perspective, conventional maneuvering models use predefined constraint polynomial bases, whereas data-driven models use data-adaptive bases. Motivated by this basis-function view, this paper presents a differentiable composite-approximation formulation, in which the polynomial-basis component and the data-adaptive basis component are treated as differentiable parts of a single predictor and calibrated jointly. A gradient-based co-calibration method is developed for full-scale AUV maneuvering prediction, where a sensitivity-aware mechanism regulates bounded polynomial updates while the neural residual captures remaining nonlinear discrepancies under a shared prediction objective. To account for ocean-current effects in field data, a turning-motion-based current estimation and compensation procedure is incorporated to construct current-compensated learning targets for training and rollout. The framework is evaluated using sea-trial data collected from a 7-meter AUV under multiple maneuvering conditions. Results show that the proposed method improves recursive trajectory and velocity prediction compared with polynomial-only, neural-only, and frozen-prior hybrid baselines, demonstrating its applicability to field-data-based AUV maneuvering modeling.

</details>


### [214] [A Categorial and Sheaf-Theoretic Semantics for Autonomic Component Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19525)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用范畴论和层论为SCEL（软件组件合奏语言）构建多层次数学模型，将机器人社会形式化为拓扑空间上的层，其中组件为点、合奏为开集、分布式知识为层数据。信息共享等价于层的"胶合"操作，系统故障可量化为拓扑障碍并通过层上同调测量，从而将分布式系统验证转化为数学对象的几何分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自治代理系统（如机器人集群、网络化物理系统）对传统形式化方法构成巨大挑战，SCEL的操作语义不适用于推理全局、结构和涌现性质，需要新的数学框架来建模和分析这类系统。

Method: 采用范畴论和层论构建SCEL的多层次数学模型：将组件映射为拓扑空间的点，合奏映射为开集，分布式知识映射为层数据；计算过程（如信息共享）对应层的"胶合"操作；系统故障通过拓扑障碍和层上同调进行量化分析。

Result: 提出了一种将分布式系统验证转化为数学对象几何分析的新框架，能够用层上同调度量系统故障，从而提供鲁棒自治系统设计的结构性洞察。

Conclusion: 该层论方法将复杂分布式系统的验证问题转化为数学对象的几何分析，为理解和设计鲁棒自主系统提供了深层结构性的理论基础。

Abstract: The proliferation of large-scale, decentralized systems of autonomous agents, such as swarms of robots and networked cyber-physical systems, presents a formidable challenge to traditional formal methods. The Software Component Ensemble Language (SCEL) offers a formal model for such systems, but its operational semantics is not ideal for reasoning about global, structural, and emergent properties. This report proposes a new, multi-layered mathematical model for SCEL using category theory and sheaf theory. We argue that a society of robots described in SCEL can be formally modeled as a sheaf on a topological space, where components are points, ensembles are open sets, and distributed knowledge forms the sheaf's data. In this framework, computational processes like information sharing become equivalent to the sheaf-theoretic operation of "gluing" local data. System failures can then be understood and quantified as topological obstructions, measurable by sheaf cohomology. This approach transforms the verification of a complex distributed system into the analysis of the geometry of a mathematical object, providing deep, structural insights for the design of robust autonomic systems.

</details>


### [215] [SCAN-Planner: Spatial Collision-Aware Local Planning for Route-Guided Long-Range Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19555)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yiwen Fu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAN-Planner提出了一种空间碰撞感知的局部规划框架，通过偏航感知双圆柱足迹建模机器人细长体形、投影A*搜索生成无碰撞引导以及机器人中心滑动地图机制，实现了四足机器人在狭窄通道、杂乱室内和3D非结构化环境中的安全、平滑且高效的长时间导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部规划器通常使用各向同性的几何膨胀近似机器人，或依赖平面/高程地图表示，导致在狭窄空间中的运动过于保守，且难以处理悬空结构的碰撞推理。

Method: 1) 偏航感知双圆柱足迹（yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint）建模机器人细长体形，在膨胀3D占用地图中通过稀疏查询实现全身碰撞评估；2) 投影A*搜索（projected A* search）在插值的地面跟随表面上生成无碰撞引导，通过z梯度抑制实现水平避障并保持垂直稳定性；3) 机器人中心滑动地图（robot-centric sliding map）结合边界回退策略，提供高分辨率局部碰撞检测和局部死胡同恢复。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，SCAN-Planner在密集杂乱环境、3D非结构化场景、楼梯穿越和长时间导航任务中均能生成安全、平滑且高效的轨迹。

Conclusion: SCAN-Planner通过空间碰撞感知的局部规划框架，有效解决了四足机器人在复杂3D环境中的全身碰撞推理和长时间导航问题，在狭窄空间和悬空结构场景下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to navigate through narrow passages, cluttered indoor scenes, and large-scale 3D unstructured environments. Existing local planners commonly approximate the robot using isotropic geometric inflation or rely on planar and elevation-map representations, leading to conservative motion in tight spaces and limited reasoning about overhanging structures. This letter presents SCAN-Planner, a spatial collision-aware local planning framework for long-range quadruped navigation. A yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint is used to model the elongated robot body, enabling whole-body collision evaluation through sparse queries in an inflated 3D occupancy map. We further introduce a projected A* search that generates collision-free guidance on an interpolated ground-following surface, with z-gradient suppression to avoid obstacles horizontally while maintaining vertical stability. For large-scale deployment, a robot-centric sliding map with boundary fallback provides high-resolution local collision checking and recovery from local dead ends. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCAN-Planner generates safe, smooth, and efficient trajectories in dense clutter, 3D unstructured scenes, stair traversal, and long-range navigation tasks.

</details>


### [216] [One Demo is Worth a Thousand Trajectories: Action-View Augmentation for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19586)
*Chuer Pan,Litian Liang,Dominik Bauer,Eric Cousineau,Benjamin Burchfiel,Siyuan Feng,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向鱼眼相机夹爪的数据增强框架，利用高斯泼溅重建3D场景并优化轨迹，生成视觉真实且物理可行的图像-动作序列，提升机械臂操作策略在场景变化和障碍物存在时的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略对机器人初始构型和未见障碍物敏感，微小变化易导致分布外观测和执行失败，而大量数据收集成本高昂，亟需高效的数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 提出适配广角鱼眼相机的高斯泼溅公式，用于3D场景重建与编辑（添加未见物体）；2) 通过轨迹优化生成平滑、无碰撞、利于渲染新视图的动作轨迹；3) 从新视角渲染视觉观测，与优化后的动作轨迹配对生成增强数据。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该增强框架在原始场景和包含障碍物的增强场景中均显著提升了多种操作任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 该数据增强框架能有效提升视觉运动策略对场景变化的鲁棒性，尤其在需要避障的场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for manipulation have demonstrated remarkable potential in modeling complex robotic behaviors, yet minor alterations in the robot's initial configuration and unseen obstacles easily lead to out-of-distribution observations. Without extensive data collection effort, these result in catastrophic execution failures. In this work, we introduce an effective data augmentation framework that generates visually realistic fisheye image sequences and corresponding physically feasible action trajectories from real-world eye-in-hand demonstrations, captured with a portable parallel gripper with a single fisheye camera. We introduce a novel Gaussian Splatting formulation, adapted to wide FoV fisheye cameras, to reconstruct and edit the 3D scene with unseen objects. We utilize trajectory optimization to generate smooth, collision-free, view-rendering-friendly action trajectories and render visual observations from corresponding novel views. Comprehensive experiments in simulation and the real world show that our augmentation framework improves the success rate for various manipulation tasks in both the same scene and the augmented scene with obstacles requiring collision avoidance.

</details>


### [217] [Fail-RAG : A Retrieval Augmented Generation Informed Framework for Robot Failure Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19598)
*Ameya Salvi,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Fail-RAG，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的机器人操作故障检测框架，通过将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入并与故障数据库进行相似度查询，再利用视觉语言模型（VLM）分析故障细节。在仓库自动化场景的固定臂和移动机械臂实验中，Fail-RAG相比现成VLM平均故障检测准确率提高25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库自动化中机器人面临动态环境和任务导致的意外事件（故障），传统基于规则的检测方法无法适应故障形态的变化，需要一种能动态检索和利用历史故障信息的高效检测方法。

Method: 提出Fail-RAG框架：将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入向量，通过计算相似度与故障数据库进行检索匹配，再使用视觉语言模型（VLM）按照指令模板对检索到的故障进行详细分析，实现故障检测。

Result: 在固定机器人手臂和移动机械臂上进行的仿真与物理实验中，针对仓库自动化常见任务的五种机器人操作类型，Fail-RAG的平均故障检测准确率比直接使用现成VLM高出25个百分点。

Conclusion: Fail-RAG通过RAG架构有效融合了历史故障知识与VLM的分析能力，在动态变化的仓库环境中显著提升了机器人故障检测的准确性和鲁棒性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Industry automation is witnessing an evolution in robotics driven by both technological breakthroughs and societal changes: progress towards generalist robots, embodied and physical artificial intelligence (AI), and increasing labor shortage in manufacturing.An intelligent autonomous robot needs to not only act according to planned motions but also react to any unexpected events. In this study, we focus on such unexpected events in warehouses where robots are used for material handling. Specifically, we refer to any unexpected events as failures and develop methods to detect robot operations related failures. Rule-based detection methods may break since the form of failures could change due to the dynamic nature of both environments and tasks. We propose 'Fail-RAG', a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)-based failure detection framework where failure images and context information are embedded and queried against a failure database by calculating their similarities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are further used to analyze failures and provide details by following our instruction template. We evaluated the performance of Fail-RAG by conducting both simulation and physical experiments using fixed robot arms and a mobile manipulator for multiple tasks that are common in warehouse automation. Fail-RAG achieved 25 percentage point higher failure detection accuracy on average across five types of robot operations compared to using off-the-shelf VLMs, indicating its effectiveness for real-world failure detection.

</details>


### [218] [Formal Verification of Learned Multi-Agent Communication Policies via Decision Tree Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19632)
*Ahmad Farooq,Kamran Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端多智能体强化学习通信策略安全验证框架，通过策略抽象（决策树蒸馏+PRISM模型检验）实现形式化安全验证，在5-7智能体无人机编队场景中验证了18个时序逻辑属性，碰撞概率0.3%低于1%阈值，验证结果以<=0.6百分点偏差迁移至原始神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在无人机群和自动驾驶车队等安全关键场景中缺乏形式化安全保证，现有神经策略无法提供可验证的安全保障。

Method: 四阶段流水线：领域特化特征提取→决策树蒸馏（97.9% fidelity）→自动翻译为PRISM规范→基于PCTL的组合验证（成对分解+并界聚合+经验邻居建模）。

Result: VQ-VIB策略：88.9%属性满足率，所有5个安全阈值通过（碰撞概率0.3% vs 1%阈值），蒙特卡洛验证偏差<=0.6百分点；离散消息相比连续方法fidelity提升+11.6~+13.6百分点，验证加速3-4倍。

Conclusion: 该框架为深度MARL与形式化安全工作流之间提供了实用桥梁，实现了经验验证的安全属性迁移至原始网络，可用于多机器人安全部署。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables agents to develop coordination strategies through emergent communication, but neural policies lack the formal safety guarantees required for safety-critical robotic deployment in drone swarms and autonomous vehicle fleets. We present the first end-to-end framework for safety verification of learned multi-agent communication policies through policy abstraction: neural policies are distilled into interpretable decision trees, then formally verified, with empirical validation confirming that verified safety properties transfer to original networks. Our four-stage pipeline consists of domain-specific feature extraction from agent observations, decision tree distillation achieving 97.9% +/- 1.2% fidelity to neural policies, automated translation to PRISM probabilistic model checker specifications with complete feature-to-state-variable correspondence, and compositional verification of Probabilistic Computation Tree Logic (PCTL) properties via pairwise decomposition with union-bound aggregation and empirical neighbor modeling. Evaluating Vector-Quantized Variational Information Bottleneck (VQ-VIB) policies for multi-drone coordination with 5-7 agents, we verify 18 temporal logic properties across safety, liveness, and cooperation, achieving 88.9% property satisfaction with all five safety thresholds satisfied (0.3% collision probability vs. 1% threshold). Monte Carlo validation of original neural policies confirms that verified safety properties transfer with <=0.6 percentage-point deviation (95% CI). Discrete VQ-VIB messages provide +11.6 to +13.6 percentage-point fidelity advantages over continuous methods, enabling 3-4x faster verification. Our framework provides empirically validated safety verification for distilled policy abstractions, serving as a practical bridge between deep MARL and formal safety workflows for multi-robot deployment.

</details>


### [219] [CTS-MoE: Implicit Terrain Adaptation via Mixture-of-Experts for Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19633)
*Francisco Affonso,Matheus P. Angarola,Ana Luiza Mineiro,Aditya Potnis,Marcelo Becker,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CTS-MoE，一种基于密集混合专家架构与多评论家的四足机器人运动控制方法，通过感知驱动的门控机制实现跨不连续地形的自适应步态，在仿真和实物实验中优于单一策略和分层策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不连续地形（如楼梯、缝隙、障碍）上的四足运动需要自适应行为，单一保守步态无法应对突变的拓扑变化。多任务强化学习在共享与分离之间存在张力——任务共享运动基础但奖励冲突，已有工作要么牺牲专门化（单一策略），要么牺牲泛化（分层子策略）。

Method: 提出CTS-MoE，包含：(1) 密集混合专家（MoE）actor与基于感知的门控，用于组合共享行为；(2) 多评论家架构与任务特定值头，防止值干扰。采用单阶段并发教师-学生训练，处理部分可观测性，避免顺序蒸馏。部署时路由仅依赖感知，无需高层选择器或地形分类器。

Result: 在Unitree Go1仿真和实物硬件上，在已见和未见地形上的实验表明：任务感知的专门化，更低的跟踪误差和更高的成功率，优于单一策略基线。

Conclusion: CTS-MoE通过MoE架构与多评论家机制有效解决了多任务四足运动中的共享-分离矛盾，实现了跨不连续地形的自适应感知运动控制。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion over discontinuous terrain (e.g., stairs, gaps, and obstacles) requires adaptive behavior, as a single conservative gait cannot produce the anticipatory maneuvers needed for abrupt topology changes. Cast as multi-task reinforcement learning, this problem introduces a tension between sharing and separation. Tasks use a common locomotion base but have conflicting rewards, so a policy must share behavior while avoiding value interference. Prior work addresses only one side, with monolithic policies sacrificing specialization and hierarchical sub-policies sacrificing generalization across transitions and unseen terrain. We propose CTS-MoE, which combines a dense mixture-of-experts actor with perception-based gating to compose shared behaviors and a multi-critic with task-specific value heads to prevent interference.
